function AttackMultipleTargets(W, ...)

		return;
	end;

			return;
		end;
		local a = {};
		for W, u in pairs(W) do
			local d = u:FindFirstChild(Pw(-393234 + 348153));
			if d then
				table[Pw(840630 + -885079)](a, { u, d });
			end;
		end;

			return;
		end;
		RegisterAttack:FireServer(364847 - 364847);
		RegisterHit:FireServer(a[1010950 + -1010949][1418987906 % 7547808], a);
	end);
end;

function StopPlayerAnimations(...)
	local W = player[Pw(-343804 + 298720)];
	if not W then
		return;
	end;

		return;
	end;

			a:Stop(1762580960 % 7868665);
		end;
	end;
end;

function EnableESP(...)

		return;
	end;
	x = true;
	for W, a in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		createESP(a);
	end;
	task[Pw(-496998 + 452372)](function(...)
		while x do
			pcall(updateESP);
			task[Pw(-304438 - (-259663))](R);
		end;
	end);
end;
function DisableESP(...)

		removeESP(W);
	end;
	B = {};

function getClosestPlayer(W, ...)
	local a = player[Pw(-88794 - (-43710))] and player[Pw(-989414 + 944330)]:FindFirstChild(Pw(-572054 + 527400));
	if not a then
		return nil;
	end;

		local W = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if W and (W[Pw(-326703 - (-281619))] and W[Pw(399548 + -444632)]:FindFirstChild(Pw(-395966 + 351312))) then
			local a = W[Pw(-468542 + 423458)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(Pw(417261 + -461720));
			if a and a[Pw(-28234 + -16279)] > 886640216 % 15832861 then
				return W[Pw(-494557 + 449473)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local d, X = nil, math[Pw(547977 - 592409)];
	for W, j in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if j ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[j[Pw(209365 + -254478)]] and (j[Pw(-149524 - (-104440))] and j[Pw(-830916 - (-785832))]:FindFirstChild(Pw(-1025058 - (-980404))))) then
			local W = j[Pw(300115 - 345199)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(Pw(374352 - 418811));
			if W and W[Pw(444950 + -489463)] > 200234 - 200234 then
				local W = ((j[Pw(-46268 - (-1184))][Pw(-765888 + 721234)][Pw(-767009 - (-722475))] - a[Pw(823814 + -868348)]))[Pw(-12235 - 32899)];

		end;
	end;
	return d;
end;

function ClearAura(...)
	for W, a in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if a and a[Pw(385023 + -429782)] then
			a:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;
function ApplyAura(W, ...)
	ClearAura();
	if not W then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	local a = AURA_DEFS[W];
	if not a then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	ActiveAura = W;
	local u = player[Pw(880512 - 925596)];
	if not u then
		return;
	end;
	local d = u:FindFirstChild(Pw(-782795 + 738141));
	if not d then
		return;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		local W = Instance[Pw(173927 + -218464)](Pw(396066 - 440584));
		W[Pw(-550812 + 505699)] = Pw(-144325 + 99614);
		W[Pw(606187 - 650712)] = Pw(-438596 - (-394043));
		W[Pw(-810568 - (-765743))] = a[Pw(316381 + -361206)];
		W[Pw(-130102 + 85342)] = a[Pw(452905 + -497665)];
		W[Pw(-702816 + 657820)] = a[Pw(666026 - 711022)];
		W[Pw(134608 + -179237)] = a[Pw(-631266 - (-586637))];

		return false;
	end;
	return true;
end;
